FILE: src\pages\admin\competitions\Friendlies.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '../../../context/AuthContext'
import GlassCard from '../../../components/GlassCard'
import { competitionManagementAPI } from '../../../api'
import toast from 'react-hot-toast'

export default function Friendlies() {
  const { api } = useAuth()
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState([])
  const [friendlies, setFriendlies] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    home_team_id: '',
    away_team_id: '',
    match_date: '',
    venue: '',
    series_name: '',
    season_name: '',
    gender: 'mixed',
    age_group: '',
    is_ticketed: false
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTeams()
    fetchFriendlies()
  }, [])

  const fetchTeams = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/api/teams?limit=200')
      setTeams(res.data)
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to load teams')
    }
  }

  const fetchFriendlies = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/api/competitions-management/friendlies')
      setFriendlies(res.data)
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to load friendlies')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const resetForm = () => {
    setFormData({
      home_team_id: '',
      away_team_id: '',
      match_date: '',
      venue: '',
      series_name: '',
      season_name: '',
      gender: 'mixed',
      age_group: '',
      is_ticketed: false
    })
    setEditing(null)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const payload = { ...formData }
      if (!payload.series_name) delete payload.series_name
      if (!payload.season_name) delete payload.season_name
      if (!payload.age_group) delete payload.age_group
      
      await api.post('/api/competitions-management/friendlies', payload)
      toast.success('Friendly match created')
      setShowForm(false)
      resetForm()
      fetchFriendlies()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to create friendly')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center py-20"><div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin" /></div>

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-1">Friendly Matches</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-400">Create and manage friendly matches</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            resetForm()
            setShowForm(true)
          }}
          className="bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:opacity-80 transition"
        >
          + New Friendly
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Modal form */}
      {showForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4">
          <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 p-6 w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Create Friendly Match</h2>
              <button onClick={() => { setShowForm(false); resetForm() }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-2xl">&times;</button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <select
                  className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                  value={formData.home_team_id}
                  onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, home_team_id: e.target.value })}
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Home Team</option>
                  {teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
                </select>
                <select
                  className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                  value={formData.away_team_id}
                  onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, away_team_id: e.target.value })}
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Away Team</option>
                  {teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
                </select>
              </div>
              <input
                type="datetime-local"
                className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                value={formData.match_date}
                onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, match_date: e.target.value })}
                required
              />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Venue"
                className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                value={formData.venue}
                onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, venue: e.target.value })}
              />

              <div className="border-t border-white/10 pt-4 mt-2">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">Friendly Series (Optional)</p>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Series Name"
                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                    value={formData.series_name}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, series_name: e.target.value })}
                  />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Season Name"
                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                    value={formData.season_name}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, season_name: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-3">
                  <select
                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                    value={formData.gender}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, gender: e.target.value })}
                  >
                    <option value="male">Male</option>
                    <option value="female">Female</option>
                    <option value="mixed">Mixed</option>
                  </select>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Age Group"
                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
                    value={formData.age_group}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, age_group: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={formData.is_ticketed}
                  onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, is_ticketed: e.target.checked })}
                />
                Ticketed (available for purchase)
              </label>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => { setShowForm(false); resetForm() }} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-600">
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white hover:opacity-80">
                  {submitting ? 'Saving...' : 'Create'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {friendlies.length === 0 ? (
        <GlassCard className="p-6 text-center text-gray-400">No friendlies yet.</GlassCard>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {friendlies.map(match => {
            const home = teams.find(t => t.id === match.home_team_id)
            const away = teams.find(t => t.id === match.away_team_id)
            return (
              <GlassCard key={match.id} className="p-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-bold text-white">{home?.name || 'Unknown'} vs {away?.name || 'Unknown'}</p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{new Date(match.match_date).toLocaleString()}</p>
                    {match.venue && <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{match.venue}</p>}
                    {match.is_ticketed && <span className="text-xs bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 px-2 py-1 rounded-full mt-1 inline-block">🎟️ Ticketed</span>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    {match.status === 'finished' ? (
                      <span className="text-faz-orange font-bold">{match.home_score} - {match.away_score}</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Scheduled</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </GlassCard>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: src\pages\admin\competitions\SeasonDetail.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback, useMemo, memo } from 'react'
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import GlassCard from '../../../components/GlassCard'
import api from '../../../api'
import toast from 'react-hot-toast'

// ---- Memoized Modal Forms to prevent re-render focus loss ----
const StageForm = memo(({ formData, setFormData, onSubmit, onCancel, editing, submitting }) => (
  <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    <input
      type="text"
      placeholder="Stage Name (e.g., Regular Season, Group Stage)"
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.name || ''}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
      required
    />
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <input
        type="number"
        placeholder="Order (e.g., 1, 2, 3)"
        className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
        value={formData.stage_order || 0}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, stage_order: parseInt(e.target.value) })}
      />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mt-2">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-gray-300">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={formData.is_group_stage || false}
            onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, is_group_stage: e.target.checked })}
          />
          Group Stage
        </label>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-gray-300">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={formData.is_knockout || false}
            onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, is_knockout: e.target.checked })}
          />
          Knockout
        </label>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4">
      <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-600">
        Cancel
      </button>
      <button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white hover:opacity-80">
        {submitting ? 'Saving...' : (editing ? 'Update' : 'Create')}
      </button>
    </div>
  </form>
))

const GroupForm = memo(({ formData, setFormData, onSubmit, onCancel, editing, submitting }) => (
  <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    <input
      type="text"
      placeholder="Group Name (e.g., Group A, Group B)"
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.name || ''}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
      required
    />
    <input
      type="number"
      placeholder="Order (e.g., 1, 2, 3)"
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.group_order || 0}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, group_order: parseInt(e.target.value) })}
    />
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4">
      <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-600">
        Cancel
      </button>
      <button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white hover:opacity-80">
        {submitting ? 'Saving...' : (editing ? 'Update' : 'Create')}
      </button>
    </div>
  </form>
))

const MatchForm = memo(({ formData, setFormData, onSubmit, onCancel, editing, submitting, teams, stageGroups, stage }) => (
  <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <select
        className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
        value={formData.home_team_id || ''}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, home_team_id: e.target.value })}
        required
      >
        <option value="">Home Team</option>
        {teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
      </select>
      <select
        className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
        value={formData.away_team_id || ''}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, away_team_id: e.target.value })}
        required
      >
        <option value="">Away Team</option>
        {teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
      </select>
    </div>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <input
        type="datetime-local"
        className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
        value={formData.match_date || ''}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, match_date: e.target.value })}
        required
      />
      <input
        type="text"
        placeholder="Venue (e.g., National Heroes Stadium)"
        className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
        value={formData.venue || ''}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, venue: e.target.value })}
      />
    </div>
    {stage?.is_group_stage && stageGroups.length > 0 && (
      <select
        className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
        value={formData.group_id || ''}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, group_id: e.target.value })}
      >
        <option value="">No Group</option>
        {stageGroups.map(g => <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>{g.name}</option>)}
      </select>
    )}
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        checked={formData.is_ticketed || false}
        onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, is_ticketed: e.target.checked })}
      />
      Ticketed (available for purchase)
    </label>
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4">
      <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-600">
        Cancel
      </button>
      <button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white hover:opacity-80">
        {submitting ? 'Saving...' : (editing ? 'Update' : 'Create')}
      </button>
    </div>
  </form>
))

const RuleForm = memo(({ formData, setFormData, onSubmit, onCancel, editing, submitting, stages }) => (
  <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    <select
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.from_stage_id || ''}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, from_stage_id: e.target.value })}
      required
    >
      <option value="">From Stage</option>
      {stages.map(s => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name}</option>)}
    </select>
    <select
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.to_stage_id || ''}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, to_stage_id: e.target.value })}
      required
    >
      <option value="">To Stage</option>
      {stages.map(s => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name}</option>)}
    </select>
    <select
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.rank_condition || 'winner'}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, rank_condition: e.target.value })}
    >
      <option value="winner">Winner</option>
      <option value="runner_up">Runner-up</option>
      <option value="best_runner_up">Best Runner-up</option>
      <option value="top_3">Top 3</option>
      <option value="top_4">Top 4</option>
    </select>
    <input
      type="number"
      placeholder="Number of Teams to Qualify (e.g., 1, 2)"
      className="w-full bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white"
      value={formData.number_of_teams || 1}
      onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, number_of_teams: parseInt(e.target.value) })}
      min="1"
    />
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4">
      <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-600">
        Cancel
      </button>
      <button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white hover:opacity-80">
        {submitting ? 'Saving...' : (editing ? 'Update' : 'Create')}
      </button>
    </div>
  </form>
))

// ---- Main Component ----
export default function SeasonDetail() {
  const { competitionId, seasonId } = useParams()

  const [season, setSeason] = useState(null)
  const [stages, setStages] = useState([])
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState([])
  const [matches, setMatches] = useState([])
  const [standings, setStandings] = useState(null)
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('stages')
  const [selectedStage, setSelectedStage] = useState(null)

  // Modal states
  const [showStageForm, setShowStageForm] = useState(false)
  const [showGroupForm, setShowGroupForm] = useState(false)
  const [showMatchForm, setShowMatchForm] = useState(false)
  const [showRuleForm, setShowRuleForm] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({})
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAllData()
  }, [seasonId])

  const fetchAllData = async () => {
    try {
      const [seasonRes, stagesRes, matchesRes, standingsRes, teamsRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}`),
        api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/stages`),
        api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/matches`),
        api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/standings`),
        api.get('/teams?limit=200')
      ])
      setSeason(seasonRes.data)
      setStages(stagesRes.data)
      setMatches(matchesRes.data)
      setStandings(standingsRes.data)
      setTeams(teamsRes.data)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Fetch error:', error)
      toast.error('Failed to load data')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const fetchGroups = async (stageId) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}/groups`)
      setGroups(res.data)
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to load groups')
    }
  }

  // Reset forms
  const resetStageForm = () => {
    setFormData({ name: '', stage_order: 0, is_knockout: false, is_group_stage: false })
    setEditing(null)
  }
  const resetGroupForm = () => {
    setFormData({ name: '', group_order: 0 })
    setEditing(null)
  }
  const resetMatchForm = () => {
    setFormData({
      home_team_id: '',
      away_team_id: '',
      match_date: '',
      venue: '',
      group_id: '',
      is_ticketed: false
    })
    setEditing(null)
  }
  const resetRuleForm = () => {
    setFormData({
      from_stage_id: '',
      to_stage_id: '',
      rank_condition: 'winner',
      number_of_teams: 1
    })
    setEditing(null)
  }

  // ---- Stage CRUD ----
  const handleStageSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      if (editing) {
        await api.put(`/competitions-management/stages/${editing.id}`, formData)
        toast.success('Stage updated')
      } else {
        await api.post(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/stages`, formData)
        toast.success('Stage created')
      }
      setShowStageForm(false)
      resetStageForm()
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to save')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  const handleDeleteStage = async (stageId) => {
    if (!confirm('Delete this stage and its groups/matches?')) return
    try {
      await api.delete(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}`)
      toast.success('Stage deleted')
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to delete')
    }
  }

  const openStageEdit = (stage) => {
    setEditing(stage)
    setFormData({
      name: stage.name,
      stage_order: stage.stage_order || 0,
      is_knockout: stage.is_knockout || false,
      is_group_stage: stage.is_group_stage || false
    })
    setShowStageForm(true)
  }

  // ---- Group CRUD ----
  const handleGroupSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      if (editing) {
        await api.put(`/competitions-management/groups/${editing.id}`, formData)
        toast.success('Group updated')
      } else {
        await api.post(`/competitions-management/stages/${selectedStage}/groups`, formData)
        toast.success('Group created')
      }
      setShowGroupForm(false)
      resetGroupForm()
      fetchGroups(selectedStage)
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to save')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  const handleDeleteGroup = async (groupId) => {
    if (!confirm('Delete this group and its standings/matches?')) return
    try {
      await api.delete(`/competitions-management/groups/${groupId}`)
      toast.success('Group deleted')
      fetchGroups(selectedStage)
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to delete')
    }
  }

  // ---- Match CRUD ----
  const handleMatchSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const payload = { ...formData }
      if (!payload.group_id) delete payload.group_id

      if (editing) {
        await api.put(`/competitions-management/matches/${editing.id}`, payload)
        toast.success('Match updated')
      } else {
        await api.post(`/competitions-management/stages/${selectedStage}/matches`, payload)
        toast.success('Match created')
      }
      setShowMatchForm(false)
      resetMatchForm()
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to save')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  const handleDeleteMatch = async (matchId) => {
    if (!confirm('Delete this match?')) return
    try {
      await api.delete(`/competitions-management/matches/${matchId}`)
      toast.success('Match deleted')
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to delete')
    }
  }

  const openMatchEdit = (match) => {
    setEditing(match)
    setFormData({
      home_team_id: match.home_team_id,
      away_team_id: match.away_team_id,
      match_date: match.match_date?.slice(0, 16) || '',
      venue: match.venue || '',
      group_id: match.group_id || '',
      is_ticketed: match.is_ticketed || false
    })
    setShowMatchForm(true)
  }

  const updateMatchScore = async (matchId, home_score, away_score) => {
    try {
      await api.put(`/competitions-management/matches/${matchId}`, {
        home_score,
        away_score,
        status: 'finished'
      })
      toast.success('Score updated & standings recalculated')
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to update score')
    }
  }

  // ---- Qualification Rules ----
  const handleRuleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      if (editing) {
        await api.put(`/competitions-management/qualification-rules/${editing.id}`, formData)
        toast.success('Rule updated')
      } else {
        await api.post('/competitions-management/qualification-rules', formData)
        toast.success('Rule created')
      }
      setShowRuleForm(false)
      resetRuleForm()
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to save')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  const handleDeleteRule = async (ruleId) => {
    if (!confirm('Delete this qualification rule?')) return
    try {
      await api.delete(`/competitions-management/qualification-rules/${ruleId}`)
      toast.success('Rule deleted')
      fetchAllData()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Failed to delete')
    }
  }

  // ---- Render helpers ----
  const renderStages = () => (
    <div className="pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Stages</h3>
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            resetStageForm()
            setShowStageForm(true)
          }}
          className="bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:opacity-80 transition"
        >
          + Add Stage
        </button>
      </div>

      {stages.map(stage => (
        <GlassCard key={stage.id} className="p-4">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-white">{stage.name}</h4>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-1">
                <span className={`px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded-full ${stage.is_group_stage ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400' : 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400'}`}>
                  {stage.is_group_stage ? 'Group Stage' : 'Knockout'}
                </span>
                <span className={`px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded-full ${stage.is_knockout ? 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400' : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400'}`}>
                  {stage.is_knockout ? 'Knockout' : 'Group-based'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => openStageEdit(stage)} className="text-blue-400 hover:underline text-sm">Edit</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleDeleteStage(stage.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:underline text-sm">Delete</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          {stage.is_group_stage && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedStage(stage.id)
                  fetchGroups(stage.id)
                  resetGroupForm()
                  setShowGroupForm(true)
                }}
                className="text-sm text-green-400 hover:underline"
              >
                + Add Group
              </button>
              <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                {groups.map(group => (
                  <div key={group.id} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-gray-800/50 px-4 py-2 rounded">
                    <span className="font-medium text-white">{group.name}</span>
                    <div>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          setEditing(group)
                          setFormData({ name: group.name, group_order: group.group_order || 0 })
                          setShowGroupForm(true)
                        }}
                        className="text-blue-400 hover:underline mr-3 text-sm"
                      >
                        Edit
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteGroup(group.id)}
                        className="text-red-400 hover:underline text-sm"
                      >
                        Delete
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </GlassCard>
      ))}
    </div>
  )

  const renderMatches = () => (
    <div className="pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Matches</h3>
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            resetMatchForm()
            setSelectedStage(stages[0]?.id || '')
            setShowMatchForm(true)
          }}
          className="bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:opacity-80 transition"
        >
          + Add Match
        </button>
      </div>

      {matches.length === 0 ? (
        <GlassCard className="p-6 text-center text-gray-400">No matches yet.</GlassCard>
      ) : (
        matches.map(match => {
          const home = teams.find(t => t.id === match.home_team_id)
          const away = teams.find(t => t.id === match.away_team_id)
          const group = groups.find(g => g.id === match.group_id)

          return (
            <GlassCard key={match.id} className="p-4">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-white">{home?.name || 'TBD'}</span>
                    <span className="text-gray-400">vs</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-white">{away?.name || 'TBD'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                    {new Date(match.match_date).toLocaleString()}
                    {match.venue && ` • ${match.venue}`}
                    {group && ` • ${group.name}`}
                  </div>
                  {match.is_ticketed && <span className="text-xs bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">🎟️ Ticketed</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  {match.status === 'finished' ? (
                    <div className="text-lg font-bold text-faz-orange">
                      {match.home_score} - {match.away_score}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      className="bg-white/10 px-3 py-1 rounded hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                      onClick={() => {
                        const homeScore = prompt('Home score:', '0')
                        const awayScore = prompt('Away score:', '0')
                        if (homeScore !== null && awayScore !== null) {
                          updateMatchScore(match.id, parseInt(homeScore), parseInt(awayScore))
                        }
                      }}
                    >
                      Enter Score
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button onClick={() => openMatchEdit(match)} className="text-blue-400 hover:underline text-sm">Edit</button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleDeleteMatch(match.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:underline text-sm">Delete</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          )
        })
      )}
    </div>
  )

  const renderStandings = () => {
    if (!standings) return <GlassCard className="p-6 text-center text-gray-400">No standings yet.</GlassCard>

    if (typeof standings === 'object' && !Array.isArray(standings)) {
      return (
        <div className="pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">
          {Object.entries(standings).map(([groupName, table]) => (
            <div key={groupName}>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-lg text-white mb-3">{groupName}</h4>
              <GlassCard className="overflow-x-auto p-0">
                <table className="w-full text-sm">
                  <thead className="border-b border-white/10 bg-gray-800/50">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-white">#</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-white">Team</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">P</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">W</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">D</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">L</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">GF</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">GA</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white font-bold">Pts</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {table.map((entry, idx) => {
                      const team = teams.find(t => t.id === entry.team_id)
                      return (
                        <tr key={entry.id} className="border-b border-white/5">
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-gray-300">{idx + 1}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-white">{team?.name || 'Unknown'}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.played}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.won}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.drawn}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.lost}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.goals_for}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.goals_against}</td>
                          <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-bold text-faz-orange">{entry.points}</td>
                        </tr>
                      )
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </GlassCard>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )
    }

    return (
      <div>
        <h4 className="font-bold text-lg text-white mb-3">Overall Standings</h4>
        <GlassCard className="overflow-x-auto p-0">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="border-b border-white/10 bg-gray-800/50">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-white">#</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-white">Team</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">P</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">W</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">D</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">L</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">GF</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">GA</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white font-bold">Pts</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {standings.map((entry, idx) => {
                const team = teams.find(t => t.id === entry.team_id)
                return (
                  <tr key={entry.id} className="border-b border-white/5">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-gray-300">{idx + 1}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-white">{team?.name || 'Unknown'}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.played}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.won}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.drawn}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.lost}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.goals_for}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-300">{entry.goals_against}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-bold text-faz-orange">{entry.points}</td>
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const renderRules = () => (
    <div className="pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Qualification Rules</h3>
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            resetRuleForm()
            setShowRuleForm(true)
          }}
          className="bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:opacity-80 transition"
        >
          + Add Rule
        </button>
      </div>

      {stages.length === 0 ? (
        <GlassCard className="p-6 text-center text-gray-400">Add stages first.</GlassCard>
      ) : (
        stages.map(stage => {
          const rules = stage.qualification_rules_from || []
          return (
            <GlassCard key={stage.id} className="p-4">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-white">{stage.name}</h4>
              {rules.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">No rules from this stage.</p>
              ) : (
                <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1">
                  {rules.map(rule => {
                    const toStage = stages.find(s => s.id === rule.to_stage_id)
                    return (
                      <li key={rule.id} className="flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-800/50 px-3 py-2 rounded">
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-300">
                          {rule.rank_condition} → {toStage?.name || 'Unknown'}
                        </span>
                        <button onClick={() => handleDeleteRule(rule.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:underline text-sm">
                          Delete
                        </button>
                      </li>
                    )
                  })}
                </ul>
              )}
            </GlassCard>
          )
        })
      )}
    </div>
  )

  // ---- Modal components ----
  const ModalWrapper = ({ isOpen, onClose, title, children }) => {
    if (!isOpen) return null
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4">
        <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 p-6 w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">{title}</h2>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-2xl">&times;</button>
          </div>
          {children}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // ---- Modal renderers ----
  const StageFormModal = () => (
    <ModalWrapper isOpen={showStageForm} onClose={() => { setShowStageForm(false); resetStageForm() }} title={editing ? 'Edit Stage' : 'Create Stage'}>
      <StageForm
        formData={formData}
        setFormData={setFormData}
        onSubmit={handleStageSubmit}
        onCancel={() => { setShowStageForm(false); resetStageForm() }}
        editing={editing}
        submitting={submitting}
      />
    </ModalWrapper>
  )

  const GroupFormModal = () => (
    <ModalWrapper isOpen={showGroupForm} onClose={() => { setShowGroupForm(false); resetGroupForm() }} title={editing ? 'Edit Group' : 'Add Group'}>
      <GroupForm
        formData={formData}
        setFormData={setFormData}
        onSubmit={handleGroupSubmit}
        onCancel={() => { setShowGroupForm(false); resetGroupForm() }}
        editing={editing}
        submitting={submitting}
      />
    </ModalWrapper>
  )

  const MatchFormModal = () => {
    const stage = stages.find(s => s.id === selectedStage)
    const stageGroups = groups || []
    return (
      <ModalWrapper isOpen={showMatchForm} onClose={() => { setShowMatchForm(false); resetMatchForm() }} title={editing ? 'Edit Match' : 'Add Match'}>
        <MatchForm
          formData={formData}
          setFormData={setFormData}
          onSubmit={handleMatchSubmit}
          onCancel={() => { setShowMatchForm(false); resetMatchForm() }}
          editing={editing}
          submitting={submitting}
          teams={teams}
          stageGroups={stageGroups}
          stage={stage}
        />
      </ModalWrapper>
    )
  }

  const RuleFormModal = () => (
    <ModalWrapper isOpen={showRuleForm} onClose={() => { setShowRuleForm(false); resetRuleForm() }} title={editing ? 'Edit Rule' : 'Add Qualification Rule'}>
      <RuleForm
        formData={formData}
        setFormData={setFormData}
        onSubmit={handleRuleSubmit}
        onCancel={() => { setShowRuleForm(false); resetRuleForm() }}
        editing={editing}
        submitting={submitting}
        stages={stages}
      />
    </ModalWrapper>
  )

  // ---- Main render ----
  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center py-20"><div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin" /></div>
  if (!season) return <div className="text-center text-gray-400 pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">Season not found</div>

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 space-y-6 mx-auto px-6">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <Link to={`/admin/competitions/${competitionId}`} className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green">← Back</Link>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">{season.name}</h1>
        <span className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm ${
          season.status === 'active' ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400' :
          season.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400' : 'bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400'
        }`}>
          {season.status}
        </span>
        {season.is_current && <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400">Current</span>}
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="flex border-b border-white/10">
        {['stages', 'matches', 'standings', 'rules'].map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab)}
            className={`px-6 py-3 font-medium transition ${activeTab === tab ? 'text-faz-orange border-b-2 border-faz-orange' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'}`}
          >
            {tab.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tab.slice(1)}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4">
        {activeTab === 'stages' && renderStages()}
        {activeTab === 'matches' && renderMatches()}
        {activeTab === 'standings' && renderStandings()}
        {activeTab === 'rules' && renderRules()}
      </div>

      {/* Modals */}
      <StageFormModal />
      <GroupFormModal />
      <MatchFormModal />
      <RuleFormModal />
    </div>
  )
}
